64:["$","$Ld6",null,{"kind":"insight","title":"为什么需要意图（p.16）","children":"智能体不可能在每一次观测之后都重新考虑所有可能的目标：那样它会把全部时间花在权衡上，什么也做不完。 意图把「决定做什么」固定一段时间，只有信念发生足够大的变化（例如充电桩坏了）才重新考虑。 承诺太强会在世界变了之后还执着于过时的目标；承诺太弱会不停地改主意。阅读材料 Rao 与 Georgeff（1995）讨论了意图的承诺与重新考虑在实际系统里怎么处理。"}]

<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> observe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(goal, pos):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """o_t：以 pos 为中心的 3x3，'#' = 地图外，'G' = 目标，'.' = 空地"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="8"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    o </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {}</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="9"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">            c </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (pos[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dx, pos[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dy)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">            o[c] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span not</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">and</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> c[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span goal </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "."</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="16"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    dx, dy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cell[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> pos[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], cell[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> pos[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="19" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> pi_reactive</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(pos, o):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """a_t = π(o_t)：只看此刻。看见目标就朝它走，否则往左"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    goal </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [c </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> c, v </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> o.items() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> v </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "G"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> toward(pos, goal[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> goal </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "L"</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> U</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(b, o):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """b_t = U(b_{t-1}, a_{t-1}, o_t)：位置已按上一步动作推算好，这里把新观测并进记忆"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    b </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> dict</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(b)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    b.update(o)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> b</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> pi_model</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(pos, b):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """a_t = π(b_t)：记忆里有目标就 BFS 过去，否则去最近的「旁边还没看过」的格子"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="32"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    goal </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [c </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> c, v </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> b.items() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> v </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "G"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="33"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    parent, q </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> {pos: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">}, deque([pos])</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="34"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> q:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="35"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        cur </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> q.popleft()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="36"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        frontier </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">((cur[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dx, cur[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dy) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">not</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> b</span></span>
